@SpringBootApplication
        -> @SpringBootConfiguration
        -> @EnableAutoConfiguration
        -> @ComponentScan
 ^B22dfBhE

    - @SpringBootApplication
    This is the main class of Spring Boot Application
    Start the application from here and automatically configure the 
    required components. 
    
    - @SpringBootConfiguration 
    It tells spring that this class contains the configuration for the 
    application.

    - @EnableAutoConfiguration 
    Automatically configure the application based on the dependency 
    available in the project. ^eU1s37Yl

<dependency> 
<group-id> org.springframework.boot </group-d>
<artifactId> spring-boot-starter-web </artifactId>
</dependency> ^pKOf9ZvT

Spring Boot detect it


Automatic Configuration

Web Application Start ^IktXnQTu

-> @ComponentScan 
    
    It tells the spring to find spring components in the application 
    package.


SpringApplication.run ^5pqbWEZX

--> @Configuration 
    
   @Configuration is used on a Java class that contains bean definition.
    

    @Configuration 
     public class MyConfig{
                } ^14TvT86d

--> @Bean

 @Bean is used on a method.
  
Note: Bean internal call the method, take the return object and 
        register it in the Spring IOC container.
 ^KOLf2g1M

REST API 

- It stands for Representational state transfer application
     programming interface.
 
- In a Spring Boot, Rest API are commonly used to allow a frontend, 
    mobile app, or another application to communicate with the 
    backend using HTTP. ^YWaPXdvg

ARCHITECTURE OF SPRING BOOT REST API  ^7Zw0EvBt

User (httpRequest)


Rest Controller
 

Service Layer


 Repository Layer


 DataBase ^9vQYU5uJ

Rest API Http Method 
   
1. Get  - Read/fetch Data 
    used to retrieve data from the server 
    
    Request : Get /api/accounts/101 ^Y8d1Eflh

Response : { "account_id" : 101,
                "name" : "Raunak",
                "balance": 21273
                } ^q2eJynUJ

2. Post (Create New Data)
    used to create a new resource
    
    Request : { "name" : "Raunak", 
                    "balance" : 151
                    }
    Response : { "account_id" : 102,
                    "name" : "Raunak",
                    "balance" : 151
                        } ^WpLGdN0I

3. PUT (Update Complete Data)
    used to completely update an existing resource.
   
    Request :  PUT /api/accounts/102
    
    {"name":"Prachi",
       "balance":101
        }

4. PATCH (Update Partial Data)
    used when you want to update only some fields 

Request : PATCH /api/accounts/102

{"name":"Ram"}


5. DELETE (Delete Data)
    used to remove a resource 
   
Request : DELETE /api/account/102 ^2Hiy3jUb

@Autowired 
    
    @Autowired is used in SpringBoot for dependency injection.
    It tells spring - 
    " Find the required object (Bean) & automatically provide it here. "

 ^IIOuTMTz

@RequestParam is used to read value from the URL, Query Parameter
in springboot Rest API.
    
    URL Parameter --> @RequestParam 


for example , 
    
    GET /student?id=101
        id:Parameter name
        101: Parameter value ^Abu8uS3K

What is the difference between RequestBody and RequestParam
 ^aJdRcPp8

RequestBody ^h1Jbvehx

RequestParam ^ukS09Pe5

--> Reads data from 
JSON Body.
--> Commonly used.
--> Used with POST, 
 PUT, PATCH
-->Convert JSON into 
Java Object  ^Q9YremV9

--> Reads data from query
parameter.
--> Commonly used for small
parameters.
--> Often used with GET
--> Convert Param into
Java Value ^Y1xlZziw

@PathVariable 

    It is use to extract a value from the URL path and use it inside 
    a controller method.

--> Difference between RequestParam and PathVariable

MVC (Model-View-Controller)
 ^aLMgPM4M

 #loosely Coupling Application
 #Maintenance easier ^JJzQkIAV

MVC 

    It is a software design pattern use to separate on application data,
user interface and business request handling logic.

- In Spring Boot MVC, It is commonly used to develop web application 
such as enterprise application.

--> Architecture of MVC
MVC has three main component
 ^mH1GyDAS

View ^effG6TAb

     Controller
handles request and 
select the response ^WgcYISuQ

Model 
Application Data 
and business logic ^TthXCt3i

1. Model :
    -> Represents application data
    -> Contains data objects and may work with business or 
        service-layer logic.
    -> Often uses entities, DTO (Data transfer object) 
        and repositories in Spring Boot Application.

2. View : 
    -> Display information to the user.
    -> Common technologies include HTML, CSS, Javascript and Thymleaf.

3. Controller : 
    -> Receive HTTP request from the user.
    -> Calls the service layer when business logic is needed.
    -> Return a view name or an HTTP response.

How MVC work in SpringBoot

Consider a E-Commerce System

Step 1 : User
Step 2 : Dispatcher Servlet
Step 3 : Controller
Step 4 : Service/Repository
Step 5 : View ^UlR8ist4

Task : Create a spring boot web application 
with frontend using thymleaf  in which a page accepts
Name of student
course of student
and marks 
and shows the input with conditions in another page or same 
marks > 90 --> A grade 
marks < 90 --> B grade 
marks <50 --> Fail ^ZNyvvEP8
